                hit=graphics::pick(app_.getScene(),pass.camera,event.framebufferX,event.framebufferY,{double(v.x),top,double(v.width),double(v.height)});
        }
    } else hit=app_.pickAt(event.windowX,event.windowY);
    if (hit) {
        std::cout << "entity=" << hit->entityId << " face=" << hit->faceIndex << '\n';
    }
}

/// 조작 방법을 출력한다.
void Console::printHelp() const {
    std::cout << "standard: 프레임워크 Motion·반사·레이어 샘플\n"
              << "1 타원 / 2 사각형 / 3 다각형 / 4 사용자 3D 경로+보간 / 5 원 / 6 왕복 꺾은선\n"
              << "Space 시간 정지·재개 / S 자전 기여 / G 전체 공전 기여\n"
              << "M 반사 변경 / I 크기 레이어 삽입·삭제 / H 도움말 / Esc 종료\n"
              << "초기 반사: YZ평면. M은 점대칭 -> Y축 직선대칭 -> 없음 -> YZ평면 순환\n"
              << "왼쪽 클릭: 물체의 entity/face ID 출력\n"
              << "7 Grid/거리 기반 배치 / 8 실내 미끄럼·낙하 / 0 기존 Motion 예제\n"
              << "9 선형보간·진자·사람형·그네→사람 계층 애니메이션\n"
              << "9번 관절: Tab/Backspace 선택, X/Y/Z 축, 좌우 회전, 상하 이동, PageUp/Down 피벗, +/- 균일 배율, R 복원\n"
              << "V 3뷰 전환 / D 단계 추적 로그 / F 실내 예제 바닥 개방 / Space 정지·재개\n";
}

/// 거리 기반 격자 예제를 구성한다.
bool Console::initGrid() {
    auto& scene=app_.getScene();scene.clear();demo_=Demo::Grid;clock_.reset();gridBoxes_.clear();
    graphics::GridLayout grid;grid.origin={-3,-.1f,-2};grid.extent={6,.2f,4};grid.cells={6,1,4};
    for(int z=0;z<4;++z) for(int x=0;x<6;++x) {
        const auto center=grid.cellCenter({x,0,z}),size=grid.relativeSize({.15f,1,.22f});
        if(!center || !size || !graphics::PatternSystem::addBox(scene,*size,*center,(x+z)%2?graphics::GlColor{.25f,.3f,.4f}:graphics::GlColor{.4f,.45f,.55f})) return false;
    }
    if(!distancePath_.setPoints({{-2.5f,.35f,-1.5f},{2.5f,.35f,-1.5f},{2.5f,.35f,1.5f}})) return false;
    for(unsigned i=0;i<5;++i) {
        const auto box=graphics::PatternSystem::addBox(scene,{.4f,.5f,.4f},{0,0,0},{.25f+.14f*i,.7f-.1f*i,.3f});
        if(!box || !graphics::PatternSystem::addNumber(scene,i+1,{-.07f,-.1f,.205f},.2f,{1,1,1},*box)) return false;
        gridBoxes_.push_back(*box);
    }
    app_.getCamera()=graphics::Camera{};app_.getCamera().position={7,6,8};app_.getCamera().target={0,0,0};
    app_.getCamera().orthographicHalfExtent=4;return true;
}
/// 회전하는 방 예제를 구성한다.
bool Console::initRoom() {
    auto& scene=app_.getScene();scene.clear();demo_=Demo::Room;clock_.reset();floorOpen_=false;
    const auto room=graphics::PatternSystem::addRoom(scene,{{-2,-1,-2},{2,2,2}});
    const auto cube=graphics::PatternSystem::addBox(scene,{.5f,.5f,.5f},{0,-.75f,0},{1,.7f,.2f});
    if(!room || !cube) return false;room_=*room;roomCube_=*cube;
    roomBody_=graphics::RoomBody{};roomBody_.localPosition={0,-.75f,0};roomBody_.halfExtent={.25f,.25f,.25f};
    app_.getCamera()=graphics::Camera{};app_.getCamera().position={5,3,8};app_.getCamera().target={0,0,0};
    return true;
}
/// 선형보간과 그네·사람의 부모·자식 애니메이션을 구성한다.
bool Console::initAnimation() {
    auto& scene = app_.getScene();
    scene.clear(); demo_ = Demo::Animation; clock_.reset(); animation_.clear();
    jointControls_.clear(); selectedJoint_=0; selectedAxis_=0;
    if (!graphics::PatternSystem::addBox(scene, {8, .15f, 6}, {0, -.15f, 0}, {.25f, .3f, .35f})) return false;
    graphics::SwingConfig swingConfig;
    swingConfig.pivot = {1, 3, 0};
    swingConfig.pendulum.amplitudeDegrees = 25;
    swingConfig.pendulum.periodSeconds = 3;
    const auto swing = graphics::AnimationSystem::addSwingWithRider(scene, swingConfig);
    if (!swing) return false;
    animation_ = swing->animation;

    graphics::HumanoidConfig personConfig;
    personConfig.grid.frame = glm::translate(glm::mat4(1), glm::vec3(-2, 0, 0));
    const auto person = graphics::AnimationSystem::addHumanoid(scene, personConfig);
    if (!person) return false;
    auto pose = graphics::HumanoidPose::seated();
    pose.hips = {glm::vec3(0), glm::vec3(0)};
    pose.knees = {glm::vec3(0), glm::vec3(0)};
    if (!graphics::AnimationSystem::poseHumanoid(scene, *person, pose)) return false;
    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < 2; ++i) {
        graphics::PendulumMotion sway;
        sway.amplitudeDegrees = 30; sway.periodSeconds = 2;
        sway.phaseDegrees = i == 0 ? 0 : 180;
        graphics::Motion motion;
        if (!motion.append(graphics::Motion::pendulum(sway))) return false;
        const auto track = graphics::AnimationSystem::createJointAnimation(scene, person->shoulders[i], motion);
        if (!track) return false;
        animation_.push_back(*track);
    }
    const auto marker = scene.addGroup();
    if (!marker || !graphics::PatternSystem::addBox(scene, {.4f, .4f, .4f}, {0, 0, 0}, {1, .85f, .15f}, *marker)) return false;
    graphics::LinearMotion move;
    move.from = {-2, .3f, 2}; move.to = {2, .3f, 2};
    move.durationSeconds = 4; move.playback = graphics::Playback::PingPong;
    graphics::Motion motion;
    if (!motion.append(graphics::Motion::linear(move))) return false;
    const auto track = graphics::AnimationSystem::createJointAnimation(scene, *marker, motion);
    if (!track) return false;
    animation_.push_back(*track);
    const auto control = [&](graphics::EntityId id, const std::string& name) {
        std::size_t index=0;
        while(index<animation_.size() && animation_[index].joint!=id) ++index;
        if(index==animation_.size()) {
            auto fixed=graphics::AnimationSystem::createJointAnimation(scene,id,graphics::Motion{});
            if(!fixed) return false;
            animation_.push_back(*fixed);
        }
        jointControls_.push_back({index,name,animation_[index].rest}); return true;
    };
    const auto personControls = [&](const graphics::HumanoidRig& rig,const std::string& prefix) {
        if(!control(rig.root,prefix+" root") || !control(rig.pelvis,prefix+" pelvis")
           || !control(rig.torso,prefix+" torso") || !control(rig.neck,prefix+" neck")) return false;
        for(std::size_t i=0;i<2;++i) {
            const auto side=prefix+(i==0?" left ":" right ");
            if(!control(rig.shoulders[i],side+"shoulder") || !control(rig.elbows[i],side+"elbow")
               || !control(rig.hands[i],side+"hand") || !control(rig.hips[i],side+"hip")
               || !control(rig.knees[i],side+"knee") || !control(rig.feet[i],side+"foot")) return false;
        }
        return true;
    };
    if(!control(swing->frame,"swing frame") || !control(swing->hinge,"swing hinge")
       || !control(swing->seat,"swing seat") || !personControls(swing->person,"rider")
       || !personControls(*person,"standing")) return false;
    app_.getCamera() = graphics::Camera{};
    app_.getCamera().position = {8, 5, 10}; app_.getCamera().target = {0, 1.4f, 0};
    std::cout << "9: 노란 상자는 4초 편도 선형보간, 그네는 3초 주기 진자, 좌석의 사람은 계층 변환을 따릅니다.\n";
    printSelectedJoint();
    return graphics::AnimationSystem::poseHierarchy(scene, animation_, 0);
}
/// 선택한 관절의 기준 자세를 편집하고 같은 시각의 애니메이션에 반영한다.
bool Console::handleJointKey(int key) {
    if(jointControls_.empty()) return false;
    if(key==GLFW_KEY_TAB) selectedJoint_=(selectedJoint_+1)%jointControls_.size();
    else if(key==GLFW_KEY_BACKSPACE) selectedJoint_=(selectedJoint_+jointControls_.size()-1)%jointControls_.size();
    else if(key==GLFW_KEY_X || key==GLFW_KEY_Y || key==GLFW_KEY_Z)
        selectedAxis_=key==GLFW_KEY_X?0:key==GLFW_KEY_Y?1:2;
    else {
        auto& track=animation_[jointControls_[selectedJoint_].track];
        const auto saved=track.rest;
        if(key==GLFW_KEY_LEFT || key==GLFW_KEY_RIGHT) {
            glm::vec3 axis(0);axis[selectedAxis_]=1;
            track.rest.rotation=track.rest.rotation*glm::mat3(glm::rotate(glm::mat4(1),
                glm::radians(key==GLFW_KEY_LEFT?-5.0f:5.0f),axis));
        } else if(key==GLFW_KEY_UP || key==GLFW_KEY_DOWN)
            track.rest.basis[selectedAxis_]+=key==GLFW_KEY_UP?.05f:-.05f;
        else if(key==GLFW_KEY_PAGE_UP || key==GLFW_KEY_PAGE_DOWN)
            track.rest.localBasis[selectedAxis_]+=key==GLFW_KEY_PAGE_UP?.05f:-.05f;
        else if(key==GLFW_KEY_EQUAL || key==GLFW_KEY_KP_ADD || key==GLFW_KEY_MINUS || key==GLFW_KEY_KP_SUBTRACT) {
            const bool grow=key==GLFW_KEY_EQUAL || key==GLFW_KEY_KP_ADD;
            track.rest.scale=glm::vec3(std::clamp(track.rest.scale.x*(grow?1.1f:1/1.1f),.1f,3.0f));
        } else if(key==GLFW_KEY_R) track.rest=jointControls_[selectedJoint_].initialRest;
        else return false;
        if(!graphics::AnimationSystem::poseHierarchy(app_.getScene(),animation_,clock_.seconds)) {
            track.rest=saved;
            std::cerr<<"관절 편집 거절: 기준 자세를 복원했습니다.\n";
        }
    }
    printSelectedJoint();return true;
}
/// 선택 관절·축·이동·피벗·균일 배율을 출력한다.
void Console::printSelectedJoint() const {
    if(jointControls_.empty()) return;
    const auto& control=jointControls_[selectedJoint_];
    const auto& rest=animation_[control.track].rest;
    std::cout<<"joint "<<selectedJoint_+1<<'/'<<jointControls_.size()<<": "<<control.name
        <<" axis="<<"XYZ"[selectedAxis_]<<" position="<<rest.basis.x<<','<<rest.basis.y<<','<<rest.basis.z
        <<" pivot="<<rest.localBasis.x<<','<<rest.localBasis.y<<','<<rest.localBasis.z
        <<" scale="<<rest.scale.x<<'\n';
}
/// 프레임 시간으로 상태와 장면을 갱신한다.
void Console::onUpdate(float dt) {
    if(demo_==Demo::Motion || !clock_.playing) return;
    if(!clock_.advance(dt)) {app_.requestClose();return;}
    auto& scene=app_.getScene();bool ok=true;
    if (demo_ == Demo::Animation) {
        ok = graphics::AnimationSystem::poseHierarchy(scene, animation_, clock_.seconds);
    } else if(demo_==Demo::Grid) {
        for(std::size_t i=0;i<gridBoxes_.size();++i)
            ok=graphics::PatternSystem::follow(scene,gridBoxes_[i],distancePath_,clock_.seconds+distancePath_.length()*i/5)&&ok;
    } else {
        const float angle=.4f*float(std::sin(clock_.seconds));
        const auto matrix=glm::rotate(glm::mat4(1),angle,glm::vec3(0,0,1));
        graphics::Transform3D roomTransform;roomTransform.rotation=glm::mat3(matrix);roomTransform.basis=glm::vec3(matrix*glm::vec4(0,.5f,0,1));
        ok=scene.setTransform(room_,roomTransform) && scene.setFaceVisible(room_,5,!floorOpen_)
            && graphics::CollisionSystem::stepRoomBody(roomBody_,{{-2,-1,-2},{2,2,2}},matrix,dt,floorOpen_,false,{0,-9.8f,0},{0,0,.4f*float(std::cos(clock_.seconds))});
        graphics::Transform3D cubeTransform;cubeTransform.basis=roomBody_.position(matrix);
        if(!roomBody_.released) cubeTransform.rotation=glm::mat3(matrix);
        ok=scene.setTransform(roomCube_,cubeTransform)&&ok;
    }
    if(!ok) app_.requestClose();
}
/// 현재 장면을 지정한 뷰로 렌더링한다.
bool Console::onRender() {
    if(!threeViews_) return app_.renderScene();
    const auto size=app_.getFramebufferSize();if(size[0]<3 || size[1]<2) return true;
    return app_.renderViews(graphics::PatternSystem::threeViews(app_.getCamera(),size[0],size[1],app_.getRenderOptions()));
}
실제 저장소 소스 · framework/examples/motion.cpp
#include "../Framework.h"
#include <iostream>

int main() {
    using namespace graphics;
    Application app;
    std::optional<MotionId> pathId, spinId, globalId;
    std::optional<LayerId> mirrorLayer, normalSpinLayer, insertedLayer;
    bool paused = false, spinEnabled = true, globalEnabled = false;
    int reflection = 1;

    const auto choosePath = [&](int kind) {
        PathMotion path;
        path.durationSeconds = 6;
        const glm::vec3 center(1.7f, 0, 0);
        if (kind == 1) path.path = PathSystem::ellipse(center, {.8f, 0, 0}, {0, .5f, 0});
        else if (kind == 2) path.path = PathSystem::rectangle(center, {.8f, 0, 0}, {0, .5f, 0});
        else if (kind == 3)
            path.path = PathSystem::polygon({{1, -.5f, 0}, {2.4f, -.4f, 0},
                {2.1f, .6f, 0}, {1.4f, .8f, 0}, {.9f, .2f, 0}});
        else if (kind == 4) {
            path.path = [center](double u) {
                const double angle = u * 6.283185307179586;
                return center + glm::vec3(float(.7 * std::sin(2 * angle)),
                    float(.6 * std::sin(angle)), float(.3 * std::cos(angle)));
            };
            path.interpolation = PathSystem::smoothStep;
        } else if (kind == 5) path.path = PathSystem::circle(center, .65f, {0, 0, 1});
        else {
            path.path = PathSystem::polyline({{1, -.5f, 0}, {2.4f, -.5f, 0}, {2, .6f, 0}});
            path.playback = Playback::PingPong;
        }
        Motion motion;
        auto& motions = app.getScene().getMotions();
        return pathId && motion.append(Motion::followPath(path))
            && motions.setMotion(*pathId, motion) && motions.seekMotion(*pathId, 0);
    };

    app.setInitHandler([&](Application& current) {
        auto& scene = current.getScene();
        auto& motions = scene.getMotions();
        const auto first = scene.addCuboid({.4f, .6f, .3f}, {0, 0, 0});
        const auto second = scene.addCuboid({.4f, .6f, .3f}, {0, 0, 0});
        if (!first || !second) return false;
        if (!scene.setTint(*first, {1, .35f, .2f}) || !scene.setTint(*second, {.2f, .7f, 1})) return false;
        pathId = motions.createMotion(Motion{});
        RotationMotion spin;
        spin.degreesPerSecond = 90;
        spinId = motions.createSpin(spin);
        RotationMotion global;
        global.degreesPerSecond = 15;
        globalId = motions.createOrbit(global);
        if (!pathId || !spinId || !globalId || !choosePath(1)) return false;
        if (!motions.setMotionEnabled(*globalId, false)
            || !motions.bindMotion(MotionSystem::rootLayer(), *globalId)) return false;

        const auto normalPath = motions.pushLayer(MotionSystem::rootLayer());
        const auto reflectionMatrix = TransformSystem::reflectPlane({0, 0, 0}, {1, 0, 0});
        if (!normalPath || !reflectionMatrix) return false;
        LayerConfig mirror;
        mirror.transform = *reflectionMatrix;
        mirrorLayer = motions.pushLayer(MotionSystem::rootLayer(), mirror);
        if (!mirrorLayer) return false;
        const auto mirroredPath = motions.pushLayer(*mirrorLayer);
        normalSpinLayer = motions.pushLayer(*normalPath);
        if (!mirroredPath || !normalSpinLayer) return false;
        const auto mirroredSpin = motions.pushLayer(*mirroredPath);
        if (!mirroredSpin || !motions.bindMotion(*normalPath, *pathId)
            || !motions.bindMotion(*mirroredPath, *pathId)
            || !motions.bindMotion(*normalSpinLayer, *spinId)
            || !motions.bindMotion(*mirroredSpin, *spinId)
            || !scene.bindEntity(*first, *normalSpinLayer)
            || !scene.bindEntity(*second, *mirroredSpin)) return false;
        if (!scene.addBasis({0, 0, 0}, glm::mat3(1), {.55f, .55f, .55f}, *first)
            || !scene.addBasis({0, 0, 0}, glm::mat3(1), {.55f, .55f, .55f}, *second)
            || !scene.addLine({0, -1.4f, 0}, {0, 1.4f, 0}, {.5f, .5f, .5f})) return false;
        current.getCamera().position = {5, 3, 8};
        std::cout << "1 타원, 2 사각형, 3 다각형, 4 사용자 경로+보간, 5 원, 6 왕복 꺾은선\n"
                  << "Space 시간 정지/재개, S 자전 표시, G 전체 공전, M 반사 변경, I 레이어 삽입/삭제\n"
                  << "M: 없음 -> YZ평면 반사 -> 점대칭 -> Y직선 대칭 순환. 클릭: 객체 ID. Esc 종료\n";
        return true;
    });
    app.setKeyHandler([&](Application& current, int key, int action) {
        if (action != GLFW_PRESS) return;
        auto& motions = current.getScene().getMotions();
        bool ok = true;
        if (key == GLFW_KEY_ESCAPE) current.requestClose();
        else if (key >= GLFW_KEY_1 && key <= GLFW_KEY_6) ok = choosePath(key - GLFW_KEY_1 + 1);
        else if (key == GLFW_KEY_SPACE) {
            paused = !paused;
            ok = motions.setMotionPlaying(*pathId, !paused)
                && motions.setMotionPlaying(*spinId, !paused)
                && motions.setMotionPlaying(*globalId, !paused);
        } else if (key == GLFW_KEY_S) {
            spinEnabled = !spinEnabled;
            ok = motions.setMotionEnabled(*spinId, spinEnabled);
        } else if (key == GLFW_KEY_G) {
            globalEnabled = !globalEnabled;
            ok = motions.setMotionEnabled(*globalId, globalEnabled);
        } else if (key == GLFW_KEY_M) {
            reflection = (reflection + 1) % 4;
            std::optional<glm::mat4> matrix = glm::mat4(1);
            if (reflection == 1) matrix = TransformSystem::reflectPlane({0, 0, 0}, {1, 0, 0});
            if (reflection == 2) matrix = TransformSystem::reflectPoint({0, 0, 0});
            if (reflection == 3) matrix = TransformSystem::reflectLine(AxisLine{});
            LayerConfig config;
            if (!matrix) ok = false;
            else { config.transform = *matrix; ok = motions.setLayer(*mirrorLayer, config); }
        } else if (key == GLFW_KEY_I) {
            if (insertedLayer) {
                ok = motions.removeLayer(*insertedLayer);
                if (ok) insertedLayer.reset();
            } else {
                LayerConfig config;
                config.space = TransformSpace::Local;
                const auto scale = TransformSystem::scaling(glm::vec3(1.5f));
                if (!scale) ok = false;
                else {
                    config.transform = *scale;
                    insertedLayer = motions.insertLayer(*normalSpinLayer, config);
                    ok = insertedLayer.has_value();
                }
            }
        }
        if (!ok) current.requestClose();
    });
    app.setMouseHandler([](Application& current, const MouseEvent& event) {
        if (event.button != GLFW_MOUSE_BUTTON_LEFT || event.action != GLFW_PRESS) return;
        const auto hit = current.pickAt(event.windowX, event.windowY);
        if (hit) std::cout << "entity=" << hit->entityId << " face=" << hit->faceIndex << '\n';
    });
    ApplicationConfig config;
    config.title = "Motion and layer tree";
    return app.run(config);
}

함수·반환값·실패 조건과 함께 읽기

부품 계층과 소유자 기저선

탱크 몸체에 포탑을 달고, 몸체 전체와 한 면을 따로 움직인다. 부모를 설정한 뒤 포탑 위치는 부모 좌표로 해석된다. 부모의 월드 행렬이 포탑 로컬 행렬 앞에 곱해진다.

주요 상태: root는 회전 중심만 가진 그룹, body는 표시 메시다. pose.basis는 부모 공간 위치, localBasis는 모델 안의 피벗, rotation은 회전 기저, scale은 축별 배율이다. faceIndex는 엔티티 ID와 다른 면 번호다.

실제 응용 코드 묶음

새로 작성한 사용 사례. 같은 API를 쓰는 기존 실행 소스도 아래에 전체 첨부한다.

bool placeParts(graphics::Scene& scene) {
    auto root=scene.addGroup();
    auto body=scene.addCuboid({2,1,1},{0,0,0});
    if(!root||!body||!scene.setParent(*body,*root)) return false;
    graphics::Transform3D pose; pose.basis={0,.5f,0};
    if(!scene.setTransform(*body,pose) || !scene.setPosition(*body,{0,.6f,0})) return false;
    if(!scene.setLocalBasis(*body,graphics::PivotMode::Custom,{0,0,0})) return false;
    auto world=scene.worldMatrix(*body);
    auto faceWorld=scene.faceWorldMatrix(*body,0);
    if(!world||!faceWorld) return false;
    if(!scene.setFaceVisible(*body,0,true)
       ||!scene.setFaceCullMode(*body,0,graphics::CullMode::Back)) return false;
    graphics::Transform3D facePose; facePose.basis={0,.1f,0};
    if(!scene.setFaceTransform(*body,0,facePose,true)
       ||!scene.splitFace(*body,0)||!scene.setFaceSRT(*body,0,facePose)) return false;
    if(!scene.setFaceLocalMatrix(*body,0,glm::mat4(1))) return false;
    return scene.setParent(*body,std::nullopt);
}

setParent는 월드 자세를 자동 보존하지 않는다. 마지막 분리도 기존 로컬 값을 그대로 월드 값으로 사용한다. setFaceSRT와 setFaceLocalMatrix는 서로 다른 추가 변환 층이므로 사용 목적을 나눈다.

실제 저장소 소스 · openGL_3dAssign_22/Console.cpp
#include "Console.h"
#include <algorithm>
#include <cmath>
#include <iostream>
#include <stdexcept>

namespace {
graphics::EntityId requireEntity(std::optional<graphics::EntityId> id,const char* reason) {
    if(!id)throw std::runtime_error(reason);
    return *id;
}
}
graphics::EntityId Console::box(glm::vec3 size,glm::vec3 position,graphics::EntityId parent) {
    const auto id=requireEntity(graphics::PatternSystem::addBox(
        app_.getScene(),size,position,{1,1,1},parent),"탱크 상자 생성 실패: 크기·위치·부모 ID를 확인하세요.");
    colored_.push_back(id);
    return id;
}
bool Console::initScene() {
    reset();
    std::cout << "실습 22: 같은 장면을 원근 / XZ 평면도 / XY 정면도로 표시\n"
              << "방향키 이동; d 하부 높이; t 중앙 자전; l 상부 교환\n"
              << "g 포신 반대 회전; p 깃대 반대 기울기; x/X,z/Z 카메라 이동\n"
              << "y/Y 카메라 시선 회전; r/R 공전(재입력 정지); O/o 색 변경; c 초기화; q 종료\n";
    return true;
}
void Console::reset() {
    auto& scene=app_.getScene();
    scene.clear();
    colored_.clear();
    pressed_.fill(false);
    position_=glm::vec3(0);
    lowerTime_=centralAngle_=gunAngle_=poleTime_=swapProgress_=0;
    swapDirection_=1;
    cameraX_=cameraZ_=cameraYaw_=cameraOrbit_=0;
    lowerRunning_=centralRunning_=gunRunning_=poleRunning_=swapRunning_=swapStarted_=false;
    colorVersion_=0;
    root_=requireEntity(scene.addGroup(),"탱크 루트 생성 실패.");
    central_=requireEntity(graphics::PatternSystem::addJoint(scene,root_,{0,.6f,0}),
        "중앙 관절 생성 실패.");
    const auto ground=requireEntity(scene.addGroup(),"바닥 그룹 생성 실패.");
    box({12,.1f,12},{0,-.05f,0},ground);
    for(std::size_t i=0;i<2;++i)lower_[i]=box({.65f,.6f,1.8f},{i==0?-.8f:.8f,.3f,0},root_);
    box({2.3f,.25f,1.4f},{0,.125f,0},central_);
    for(std::size_t i=0;i<2;++i) {
        const float sign=i==0?-1.0f:1.0f;
        upper_[i]=requireEntity(graphics::PatternSystem::addJoint(scene,central_,{sign*.65f,.25f,0}),
            "상부 관절 생성 실패.");
        guns_[i]=requireEntity(graphics::PatternSystem::addJoint(scene,upper_[i],{0,.3f,0}),
            "포신 중심 피벗 생성 실패.");
        poles_[i]=requireEntity(graphics::PatternSystem::addJoint(scene,upper_[i],{0,.6f,0}),
            "깃대 밑점 피벗 생성 실패.");
        box({.65f,.6f,.8f},{0,.3f,0},upper_[i]);

        box({.14f,.14f,1.1f},{0,-.05f,.95f},guns_[i]);
        box({.08f,.85f,.08f},{0,.425f,0},poles_[i]);
    }
    auto& camera=app_.getCamera();
    camera=graphics::Camera{};
    camera.position={-5,4.5f,7};
    camera.target={0,.7f,0};
    camera.orthographic=false;
    camera.orthographicHalfExtent=6.5f;
    app_.getRenderOptions()=graphics::RenderOptions{};
    app_.getRenderOptions().lighting=true;
    recolor();
    apply();
}
void Console::recolor() {
    const std::array<graphics::GlColor,10> palette{
        graphics::GlColor{.3f,.35f,.4f},{.25f,.6f,.3f},{.55f,.35f,.2f},{.2f,.55f,.75f},
        {.7f,.75f,.2f},{.9f,.4f,.15f},{1,.8f,.2f},{.4f,.7f,.8f},{.7f,.25f,.6f},{.6f,.9f,.4f}};
    for(std::size_t i=0;i<colored_.size();++i)
        if(!app_.getScene().setTint(colored_[i],palette[(i+colorVersion_)%palette.size()]))
            throw std::runtime_error("탱크 부품 색상 적용 실패.");
}
void Console::apply() {
    auto& scene=app_.getScene();
    if(!scene.setPosition(root_,position_))throw std::runtime_error("탱크 위치 적용 실패.");
    const float factor=1+.25f*std::sin(lowerTime_),height=.6f*factor;
    if(!scene.setPosition(central_,{0,height,0})
        ||!graphics::PatternSystem::rotateJoint(scene,central_,{0,1,0},centralAngle_))
        throw std::runtime_error("중앙 관절 변환 적용 실패.");
    for(std::size_t i=0;i<2;++i) {
        const float sign=i==0?-1.0f:1.0f;
        graphics::Transform3D low;
        low.basis={sign*.8f,height*.5f,0};
        low.scale={1,factor,1};
        const float a=3.14159265f*swapProgress_;
        const glm::vec3 upperPosition{sign*.65f*std::cos(a),.25f,sign*.65f*std::sin(a)};
        if(!scene.setTransform(lower_[i],low)||!scene.setPosition(upper_[i],upperPosition)
            ||!graphics::PatternSystem::rotateJoint(scene,guns_[i],{0,1,0},sign*gunAngle_)
            ||!graphics::PatternSystem::rotateJoint(scene,poles_[i],{1,0,0},sign*60*std::sin(poleTime_)))
            throw std::runtime_error("탱크 부품 변환 적용 실패.");
    }
}
void Console::onKey(int key,int action,int mods) {
    const bool upper=((mods&GLFW_MOD_SHIFT)!=0)!=((mods&GLFW_MOD_CAPS_LOCK)!=0);
    if(key==GLFW_KEY_X||key==GLFW_KEY_Z) {
        if(action==GLFW_PRESS||action==GLFW_RELEASE) {
            float& direction=key==GLFW_KEY_X?cameraX_:cameraZ_;
            direction=action==GLFW_RELEASE?0:(upper?-1.0f:1.0f);
        }
        return;
    }
    if(key>=0&&key<=GLFW_KEY_LAST) {
        if(action==GLFW_PRESS)pressed_[key]=true;
        else if(action==GLFW_RELEASE)pressed_[key]=false;
    }
    if(action!=GLFW_PRESS)return;
    const float direction=upper?1.0f:-1.0f;
    switch(key) {
    case GLFW_KEY_D:
        lowerRunning_=!lowerRunning_;
        break;
    case GLFW_KEY_T:
        centralRunning_=!centralRunning_;
        break;
    case GLFW_KEY_G:
        gunRunning_=!gunRunning_;
        break;
    case GLFW_KEY_P:
        poleRunning_=!poleRunning_;
        break;
    case GLFW_KEY_L:
        if(!swapStarted_){
            swapStarted_=true;
            swapRunning_=true;
        }
        else if(!swapRunning_&&(swapProgress_==0||swapProgress_==1)){
            swapDirection_=-swapDirection_;
            swapRunning_=true;
        }
        else swapRunning_=!swapRunning_;
        break;
    case GLFW_KEY_Y:
        cameraYaw_=cameraYaw_==direction?0:direction;
        break;
    case GLFW_KEY_R:
        cameraOrbit_=cameraOrbit_==direction?0:direction;
        break;
    case GLFW_KEY_O:
        colorVersion_=(colorVersion_+1)%10;
        recolor();
        break;
    case GLFW_KEY_C:
        reset();
        break;
    case GLFW_KEY_Q:
    case GLFW_KEY_ESCAPE:
        app_.requestClose();
        break;
    default:
        break;
    }
}
void Console::onUpdate(float dt) {
    glm::vec3 movement{float(pressed_[GLFW_KEY_RIGHT])-float(pressed_[GLFW_KEY_LEFT]),0,
        float(pressed_[GLFW_KEY_DOWN])-float(pressed_[GLFW_KEY_UP])};
    if(glm::length(movement)>0)position_+=glm::normalize(movement)*dt*1.3f;

    position_.x=std::clamp(position_.x,-3.5f,3.5f);
    position_.z=std::clamp(position_.z,-3.5f,3.5f);
    if(lowerRunning_)lowerTime_=std::fmod(lowerTime_+dt,6.283185307f);
    if(centralRunning_)centralAngle_=std::fmod(centralAngle_+45*dt,360.0f);
    if(gunRunning_)gunAngle_= std::fmod(gunAngle_+60*dt,-360.0f);
    if(poleRunning_)poleTime_=std::fmod(poleTime_+dt,6.283185307f);
    if(swapRunning_) {
        swapProgress_=std::clamp(swapProgress_+swapDirection_*dt*.5f,0.0f,1.0f);
        if(swapProgress_==0||swapProgress_==1)swapRunning_=false;
    }
    auto camera=app_.getCamera();
    const glm::vec3 delta{cameraX_*2*dt,0,cameraZ_*2*dt};
    if(!graphics::PatternSystem::moveCamera(camera,delta)
        ||(cameraYaw_!=0&&!graphics::PatternSystem::yawCamera(camera,cameraYaw_*35*dt))
        ||(cameraOrbit_!=0&&!graphics::PatternSystem::orbitCamera(camera,{{0,0,0},{0,1,0}},cameraOrbit_*25*dt)))
        throw std::runtime_error("카메라 갱신 실패: 위치·시선·회전축을 확인하세요.");
    app_.getCamera()=camera;
    apply();
}
void Console::onFocus(bool focused) {
    if(!focused){
        pressed_.fill(false);
        cameraX_=cameraZ_=0;
    }
}

bool Console::onRender() {
    const auto size=app_.getFramebufferSize();
    if(size[0]<3||size[1]<2)return true;
    const auto passes=graphics::PatternSystem::threeViews(
        app_.getCamera(),size[0],size[1],app_.getRenderOptions());
    if(passes.size()!=3)throw std::runtime_error("세 카메라 뷰 생성 실패: 프레임버퍼 크기와 카메라를 확인하세요.");
    return app_.renderViews(passes);
}
실제 저장소 소스 · framework/detail/Scene.inl
#pragma once
#include <fstream>
#include <initializer_list>
#include <iostream>
#include <sstream>
#include <utility>

namespace graphics {
namespace detail {

inline bool positiveLengths(const glm::vec3& value) {
    return finiteVector(value) && value.x > 0.0f
        && value.y > 0.0f && value.z > 0.0f;
}

inline std::uint64_t nextGeometryRevision() {
    // 장면 복사·교체 시에도 다른 형상에 같은 revision을 부여하지 않는다.
    // Scene과 Renderer는 한 렌더링 스레드에서 사용한다.
    static std::uint64_t revision = 0;
    return ++revision;
}

inline void appendFace(Polyhedron& polyhedron, GLFace shape,
                std::initializer_list<glm::vec3> vertices, GlColor color) {
    PlaneFigure face;
    face.shape = shape;
    face.localVertices = vertices;
    face.color = color;
    face.normal = glm::normalize(glm::cross(
        face.localVertices[1] - face.localVertices[0],
        face.localVertices[2] - face.localVertices[0]));
    polyhedron.faces.push_back(face);
}

} // namespace detail
using detail::finiteVector;
using detail::finiteMatrix;
using detail::validColor;
using detail::positiveLengths;
using detail::nextGeometryRevision;
using detail::appendFace;

inline Scene::Scene() {
    geometryRevision_ = nextGeometryRevision();
    polyId_.reserve(MAX_ENTITIES);
    components_.reserve(MAX_ENTITIES);
    tetraFaceId_.reserve(MAX_ENTITIES * 4);
    cubeFaceId_.reserve(MAX_ENTITIES * 6);
    pyramidFaceId_.reserve(MAX_ENTITIES * 5);
    lines_.reserve(MAX_LINES);
}

inline std::optional<EntityId> Scene::addGroup(const Transform3D& transform) {
    if(!DiagramSystem::validTransform(transform))return (Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::addGroup", "Rejected condition: !DiagramSystem::validTransform(transform)", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships."), std::nullopt);
    const auto id=components_.size();
    Component_Poly group;group.polyhedron.shape=GLShape::None;
    group.transform=transform;group.sideLengths=glm::vec3(0);group.transformOnly=true;
    components_.push_back(group);polyId_.push_back(id);
    geometryRevision_=nextGeometryRevision();return id;
}
inline bool Scene::setTint(EntityId id, GlColor color) {
    if(id>=components_.size() || !validColor(color))return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::setTint", "Rejected condition: id>=components_.size() || !validColor(color)", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id);
    components_[id].tint=color;return true;
}
inline std::optional<EntityId> Scene::addSphere(float radius,const glm::vec3& position,unsigned slices,unsigned stacks) {
    Transform3D transform;transform.basis=position;
    return push_entity(DiagramSystem::makeSphere(radius,slices,stacks),transform);
}
inline std::optional<EntityId> Scene::addCone(float radius,float height,const glm::vec3& position,unsigned slices) {
    Transform3D transform;transform.basis=position;
    return push_entity(DiagramSystem::makeCone(radius,height,slices),transform);
}

inline std::optional<EntityId> Scene::push_entity(
    const Polyhedron& polyhedron, const Transform3D& transform
) {
    if (!DiagramSystem::validPolyhedron(polyhedron)
        || !DiagramSystem::validTransform(transform)) {
        return (Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::push_entity", "Rejected condition: !DiagramSystem::validPolyhedron(polyhedron) || !DiagramSystem::validTransform(transform)", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships."), std::nullopt);
    }
    glm::vec3 minimum = polyhedron.faces[0].localVertices[0];
    glm::vec3 maximum = minimum;
    for (const PlaneFigure& face : polyhedron.faces) {
        for (const glm::vec3& vertex : face.localVertices) {
            minimum = glm::min(minimum, vertex);
            maximum = glm::max(maximum, vertex);
        }
    }
    if (!finiteVector(maximum - minimum)) return (Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::push_entity", "Rejected condition: !finiteVector(maximum - minimum)", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships."), std::nullopt);
    const EntityId id = components_.size();
    components_.push_back({ polyhedron, transform, maximum - minimum });
    polyId_.push_back(id);
    std::vector<FaceId>* faceIds = nullptr;
    if (polyhedron.shape == GLShape::Cube) faceIds = &cubeFaceId_;
    if (polyhedron.shape == GLShape::Tetra) faceIds = &tetraFaceId_;
    if (polyhedron.shape == GLShape::Pyramid) faceIds = &pyramidFaceId_;
    if (faceIds) for (std::size_t i = 0; i < polyhedron.faces.size(); ++i)
        faceIds->push_back({id, i});
    geometryRevision_ = nextGeometryRevision();
    return id;
}

inline std::optional<EntityId> Scene::addCuboid(
    const glm::vec3& sideLengths, const glm::vec3& worldPosition
) {
    Transform3D transform;
    transform.basis = worldPosition;
    return push_entity(DiagramSystem::makePolyhedron(GLShape::Cube, sideLengths),
                       transform);
}

inline std::optional<EntityId> Scene::addRightTetrahedron(
    const glm::vec3& edgeLengths, const glm::vec3& worldPosition
) {
    Transform3D transform;
    transform.basis = worldPosition;
    return push_entity(DiagramSystem::makePolyhedron(GLShape::Tetra, edgeLengths),
                       transform);
}

inline std::optional<EntityId> Scene::addPyramid(
    const glm::vec3& sideLengths, const glm::vec3& worldPosition
) {
    Transform3D transform;
    transform.basis = worldPosition;
    return push_entity(DiagramSystem::makePolyhedron(GLShape::Pyramid, sideLengths),
                       transform);
}

inline bool Scene::setFaceVisible(EntityId id, std::size_t faceIndex, bool visible) {
    if (id >= components_.size()
        || faceIndex >= components_[id].polyhedron.faces.size()) {
        return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::setFaceVisible", "Rejected condition: id >= components_.size() || faceIndex >= components_[id].polyhedron.faces.size()", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id);
    }
    components_[id].polyhedron.faces[faceIndex].visible = visible;
    return true;
}

inline bool Scene::setFaceCullMode(EntityId id,std::size_t faceIndex,CullMode mode) {
    if(id>=components_.size() || faceIndex>=components_[id].polyhedron.faces.size()
        || (mode!=CullMode::Inherit && mode!=CullMode::None && mode!=CullMode::Back && mode!=CullMode::Front))
        return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Raster,"Scene::setFaceCullMode","Invalid entity, face index or culling mode.","Check the model's face list and CullMode enum.",id);
    components_[id].polyhedron.faces[faceIndex].cull=mode;
    return true;
}
inline bool Scene::setFaceLocalMatrix(EntityId id, std::size_t faceIndex,
                               const glm::mat4& matrix) {
    if (id >= components_.size()
        || faceIndex >= components_[id].polyhedron.faces.size()
        || !finiteMatrix(matrix)) {
        return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::setFaceLocalMatrix", "Rejected condition: id >= components_.size() || faceIndex >= components_[id].polyhedron.faces.size() || !finiteMatrix(matrix)", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id);
    }
    components_[id].polyhedron.faces[faceIndex].localAnimationMatrix = matrix;
    return true;
}

inline bool Scene::setFaceVertexColors(EntityId id, std::size_t faceIndex,
                                const std::vector<GlColor>& colors) {
    if (id >= components_.size()
        || faceIndex >= components_[id].polyhedron.faces.size()) {
        return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::setFaceVertexColors", "Rejected condition: id >= components_.size() || faceIndex >= components_[id].polyhedron.faces.size()", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id);
    }
    PlaneFigure& face = components_[id].polyhedron.faces[faceIndex];
    if (!colors.empty() && colors.size() != face.localVertices.size()) {
        return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::setFaceVertexColors", "Rejected condition: !colors.empty() && colors.size() != face.localVertices.size()", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id);
    }
    for (GlColor color : colors) {
        if (!validColor(color)) {
            return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::setFaceVertexColors", "Rejected condition: !validColor(color)", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id);
        }
    }
    face.vertexColors = colors;
    geometryRevision_ = nextGeometryRevision();
    return true;
}

inline const Component_Poly* Scene::getComponent(EntityId id) const {
    return id < components_.size() ? &components_[id] : nullptr;
}

inline bool Scene::setPosition(EntityId id, const glm::vec3& worldPosition) {
    // For a parented entity this position is in parent coordinates.
    if (id >= components_.size() || !finiteVector(worldPosition)) {
        return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::setPosition", "Rejected condition: id >= components_.size() || !finiteVector(worldPosition)", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id);
    }
    auto candidate = components_[id].transform;
    candidate.basis = worldPosition;
    return setTransform(id, candidate);
}

inline bool Scene::setSideLengths(EntityId id, const glm::vec3& sideLengths) {
    if(id>=components_.size()||!positiveLengths(sideLengths))return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::setSideLengths", "Rejected condition: id>=components_.size()||!positiveLengths(sideLengths)", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id);
    const auto& c=components_[id];
    if(c.transformOnly)return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::setSideLengths", "Rejected condition: c.transformOnly", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id);
    auto replacement=c.polyhedron;
    const auto box=detail::bounds(replacement);const auto oldSize=box.second-box.first;
    if(!positiveLengths(oldSize))return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::setSideLengths", "Rejected condition: !positiveLengths(oldSize)", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id);
    const auto ratio=sideLengths/oldSize;
    if(!finiteVector(ratio))return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::setSideLengths", "Rejected condition: !finiteVector(ratio)", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id);
    // Preserve topology/materials. Resize mesh coordinates around the model pivot.
    for(auto& face:replacement.faces) {
        for(auto& vertex:face.localVertices)vertex=c.transform.localBasis+(vertex-c.transform.localBasis)*ratio;
        face.transform.localBasis=c.transform.localBasis+(face.transform.localBasis-c.transform.localBasis)*ratio;
        recalculateNormals(face);
    }
    return setModel(id,replacement);
}

inline bool Scene::setParent(EntityId id, std::optional<EntityId> parent) {
    if (id >= components_.size()) return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::setParent", "Rejected condition: id >= components_.size()", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id);
    auto ancestor = parent;
    std::size_t remaining = components_.size();
    while (ancestor) {
        if (*ancestor >= components_.size() || *ancestor == id || remaining-- == 0) return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::setParent", "Rejected condition: *ancestor >= components_.size() || *ancestor == id || remaining-- == 0", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id);
        ancestor = components_[*ancestor].parent;
    }
    components_[id].parent = parent;
    return true;
}
inline std::optional<glm::mat4> Scene::worldMatrix(EntityId id) const {
    if (id >= components_.size()) return (Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::worldMatrix", "Rejected condition: id >= components_.size()", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id), std::nullopt);
    std::vector<EntityId> chain;
    std::optional<EntityId> current = id;
    while (current) {
        if (*current >= components_.size() || chain.size() >= components_.size()) return (Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::worldMatrix", "Rejected condition: *current >= components_.size() || chain.size() >= components_.size()", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id), std::nullopt);
        chain.push_back(*current);
        current = components_[*current].parent;
    }
    std::reverse(chain.begin(), chain.end());
    LayerId layer = MotionSystem::rootLayer();
    std::size_t anchor = chain.size() - 1;
    for (std::size_t i = chain.size(); i > 0; --i) {
        const auto binding = getEntityLayer(chain[i - 1]);
        if (binding) { layer = *binding; anchor = i - 1; break; }
    }
    const auto motion = motions_.layerMatrices(layer);
    if (!motion) return (Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::worldMatrix", "Rejected condition: !motion", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id), std::nullopt);
    glm::mat4 prefix(1), suffix(1);
    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < chain.size(); ++i) {
        auto& matrix = i <= anchor ? prefix : suffix;
        matrix *= detail::transformMatrix(components_[chain[i]].transform);
    }
    const auto result = motion->parent * prefix * motion->local * suffix;
    if(!detail::finiteMatrix(result)) {Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Transform,"Scene::worldMatrix","World transform overflow after parent and motion composition.","Reduce coordinate/scale magnitudes and inspect the parent/layer chain.",id);return {};}
    return result;
}
inline std::optional<glm::mat4> Scene::faceWorldMatrix(EntityId id, std::size_t faceIndex) const {
    if (id >= components_.size() || faceIndex >= components_[id].polyhedron.faces.size()) return (Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::faceWorldMatrix", "Rejected condition: id >= components_.size() || faceIndex >= components_[id].polyhedron.faces.size()", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id), std::nullopt);
    const auto world = worldMatrix(id);
    if (!world) return (Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::faceWorldMatrix", "Rejected condition: !world", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id), std::nullopt);
    const auto& face = components_[id].polyhedron.faces[faceIndex];
    glm::mat4 child(1);
    if (face.independence)
        child = glm::translate(glm::mat4(1), face.transform.localBasis)
              * detail::transformMatrix(face.transform);
    const auto result = *world * child * face.localAnimationMatrix;
    if(!detail::finiteMatrix(result)) {Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Transform,"Scene::faceWorldMatrix","Face world transform overflow.","Inspect local face animation and entity transforms.",id);return {};}
    return result;
}
inline std::optional<glm::mat4> Scene::lineWorldMatrix(LineId id) const {
    if (id >= lines_.size()) return (Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::lineWorldMatrix", "Rejected condition: id >= lines_.size()", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id), std::nullopt);
    if (lines_[id].owner) return worldMatrix(*lines_[id].owner);
    const auto global = motions_.layerMatrices(MotionSystem::rootLayer());
    return global ? std::optional<glm::mat4>(global->parent * global->local) : std::nullopt;
}
inline bool Scene::bindEntity(EntityId id, LayerId layer) {
    if (id >= components_.size() || !motions_.getLayer(layer)) return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::bindEntity", "Rejected condition: id >= components_.size() || !motions_.getLayer(layer)", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id);
    entityLayers_[id] = layer;
    return true;
}
inline bool Scene::unbindEntity(EntityId id) {
    if (id >= components_.size()) return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::unbindEntity", "Rejected condition: id >= components_.size()", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id);
    entityLayers_.erase(id);
    return true;
}
inline std::optional<LayerId> Scene::getEntityLayer(EntityId id) const {
    if (id >= components_.size()) return {};
    const auto found = entityLayers_.find(id);
    if (found == entityLayers_.end() || !motions_.getLayer(found->second)) return {};
    return found->second;
}

inline bool Scene::setTransform(EntityId id, const Transform3D& transform) {
    if (id >= components_.size() || !DiagramSystem::validTransform(transform)) {
        return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::setTransform", "Rejected condition: id >= components_.size() || !DiagramSystem::validTransform(transform)", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id);
    }
    components_[id].transform = transform;
    return true;
}

inline bool Scene::setFaceTransform(
    EntityId id, std::size_t faceIndex,
    const Transform3D& transform, bool independence
) {
    if (id >= components_.size()
        || faceIndex >= components_[id].polyhedron.faces.size()
        || !DiagramSystem::validTransform(transform)) {
        return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::setFaceTransform", "Rejected condition: id >= components_.size() || faceIndex >= components_[id].polyhedron.faces.size() || !DiagramSystem::validTransform(transform)", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id);
    }
    PlaneFigure& face = components_[id].polyhedron.faces[faceIndex];
    face.transform = transform;
    face.independence = independence;
    return true;
}

inline std::optional<LineId> Scene::addLine(
    const glm::vec3& start, const glm::vec3& end,
    GlColor color, std::optional<EntityId> owner
) {
    if (!finiteVector(start) || !finiteVector(end)
        || !validColor(color) || (owner && getComponent(*owner) == nullptr)) {
        return (Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::addLine", "Rejected condition: !finiteVector(start) || !finiteVector(end) || !validColor(color) || (owner && getComponent(*owner) == nullptr)", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships."), std::nullopt);
    }
    const LineId id = lines_.size();
    lines_.push_back({ start, end, color, owner });
    geometryRevision_ = nextGeometryRevision();
    return id;
}

inline std::optional<LineId> Scene::addVector(
    const glm::vec3& origin, const glm::vec3& vector,
    GlColor color, std::optional<EntityId> owner
) {
    return addLine(origin, origin + vector, color, owner);
}

inline std::optional<std::array<LineId, 3>> Scene::addBasis(
    const glm::vec3& origin, const glm::mat3& basisVectors,
    const glm::vec3& axisLengths, std::optional<EntityId> owner
) {
    if (!finiteVector(origin)
        || !positiveLengths(axisLengths)
        || (owner && getComponent(*owner) == nullptr)) {
        return (Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::addBasis", "Rejected condition: !finiteVector(origin) || !positiveLengths(axisLengths) || (owner && getComponent(*owner) == nullptr)", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships."), std::nullopt);
    }
    for (int i = 0; i < 3; ++i) {
        if (!finiteVector(basisVectors[i])
            || glm::dot(basisVectors[i], basisVectors[i]) <= 0.0f
            || !finiteVector(origin + basisVectors[i] * axisLengths[i])) {
            return (Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::addBasis", "Rejected condition: !finiteVector(basisVectors[i]) || glm::dot(basisVectors[i], basisVectors[i]) <= 0.0f || !finiteVector(origin + basisVectors[i] * axisLengths[i])", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships."), std::nullopt);
        }
    }
    // 사전 검증 후 세 선을 함께 등록한다. 실패 입력은 장면을 변경하지 않는다.
    const std::array<GlColor, 3> colors{
        GlColor{ 1.0f, 0.2f, 0.2f },
        GlColor{ 0.2f, 1.0f, 0.2f },
        GlColor{ 0.2f, 0.4f, 1.0f }
    };
    std::array<LineId, 3> ids{};
    for (int i = 0; i < 3; ++i) {
        ids[i] = lines_.size();
        lines_.push_back({
            origin, origin + basisVectors[i] * axisLengths[i], colors[i], owner });
    }
    geometryRevision_ = nextGeometryRevision();
    return ids;
}

inline bool Scene::setVector(LineId id, const glm::vec3& origin, const glm::vec3& vector) {
    if (id >= lines_.size() || !finiteVector(origin)
        || !finiteVector(origin + vector)) {
        return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::setVector", "Rejected condition: id >= lines_.size() || !finiteVector(origin) || !finiteVector(origin + vector)", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id);
    }
    lines_[id].start = origin;
    lines_[id].end = origin + vector;
    geometryRevision_ = nextGeometryRevision();
    return true;
}

inline void Scene::clear() {
    motions_.clear();
    entityLayers_.clear();
    if (components_.empty() && lines_.empty()) {
        return;
    }
    polyId_.clear();
    components_.clear();
    cubeFaceId_.clear();
    tetraFaceId_.clear();
    pyramidFaceId_.clear();
    lines_.clear();
    geometryRevision_ = nextGeometryRevision();
}

inline bool DiagramSystem::validTransform(const Transform3D& transform) {
    return finiteVector(transform.basis) && finiteVector(transform.localBasis)
        && finiteVector(transform.scale)
        && finiteVector(transform.rotation[0]) && finiteVector(transform.rotation[1])
        && finiteVector(transform.rotation[2])
        && std::abs(glm::determinant(transform.rotation) - 1.0f) < 0.01f
        && glm::length(glm::transpose(transform.rotation) * transform.rotation[0] - glm::vec3(1,0,0)) < 0.01f
        && glm::length(glm::transpose(transform.rotation) * transform.rotation[1] - glm::vec3(0,1,0)) < 0.01f
        && glm::length(glm::transpose(transform.rotation) * transform.rotation[2] - glm::vec3(0,0,1)) < 0.01f
        && finiteMatrix(detail::transformMatrix(transform));
}

inline bool DiagramSystem::validPolyhedron(const Polyhedron& polyhedron) {
    if (polyhedron.faces.empty()) return false;
    for (const auto& face : polyhedron.faces)
        if (!detail::validMesh(face) || !validTransform(face.transform)) return false;
    return true;
}

inline Polyhedron DiagramSystem::makePolyhedron(GLShape shape, const glm::vec3& sideLengths) {
    Polyhedron polyhedron;
    if (!positiveLengths(sideLengths)) {
        return polyhedron;
    }
    polyhedron.shape = shape;
    if (shape == GLShape::Cube) {
        const float x = sideLengths.x * 0.5f;
        const float y = sideLengths.y * 0.5f;
        const float z = sideLengths.z * 0.5f;
        polyhedron.faces.reserve(6);
        appendFace(polyhedron, GLFace::Rect,
            { { -x, -y, z }, { x, -y, z }, { x, y, z }, { -x, y, z } },
            { .9f, .3f, .3f });
        appendFace(polyhedron, GLFace::Rect,
            { { x, -y, -z }, { -x, -y, -z }, { -x, y, -z }, { x, y, -z } },
            { .3f, .8f, .4f });
        appendFace(polyhedron, GLFace::Rect,
            { { -x, -y, -z }, { -x, -y, z }, { -x, y, z }, { -x, y, -z } },
            { .3f, .5f, .9f });
        appendFace(polyhedron, GLFace::Rect,
            { { x, -y, z }, { x, -y, -z }, { x, y, -z }, { x, y, z } },
            { .9f, .8f, .3f });
        appendFace(polyhedron, GLFace::Rect,
            { { -x, y, z }, { x, y, z }, { x, y, -z }, { -x, y, -z } },
            { .3f, .8f, .9f });
        appendFace(polyhedron, GLFace::Rect,
            { { -x, -y, -z }, { x, -y, -z }, { x, -y, z }, { -x, -y, z } },
            { .8f, .3f, .9f });
    } else if (shape == GLShape::Pyramid) {
        const float x = sideLengths.x * 0.5f;
        const float y = sideLengths.y * 0.5f;
        const float z = sideLengths.z * 0.5f;
        const glm::vec3 frontLeft{ -x, -y, z };
        const glm::vec3 frontRight{ x, -y, z };
        const glm::vec3 backRight{ x, -y, -z };
        const glm::vec3 backLeft{ -x, -y, -z };
        const glm::vec3 apex{ 0.0f, y, 0.0f };
        polyhedron.faces.reserve(5);
        // 앞, 오른쪽, 뒤, 왼쪽, 바닥. 삼각형과 사각형은 바깥쪽 winding이다.
        appendFace(polyhedron, GLFace::Triangle,
            { frontLeft, frontRight, apex }, { .9f, .3f, .3f });
        appendFace(polyhedron, GLFace::Triangle,
            { frontRight, backRight, apex }, { .3f, .5f, .9f });
        appendFace(polyhedron, GLFace::Triangle,
            { backRight, backLeft, apex }, { .3f, .8f, .4f });
        appendFace(polyhedron, GLFace::Triangle,
            { backLeft, frontLeft, apex }, { .9f, .8f, .3f });
        appendFace(polyhedron, GLFace::Rect,
            { backLeft, backRight, frontRight, frontLeft }, { .8f, .3f, .9f });
    } else if (shape == GLShape::Tetra) {
        // 원점에서 직교하는 세 모서리를 만들고, 무게중심을 로컬 원점으로 옮긴다.
        const glm::vec3 center = sideLengths * 0.25f;
        const glm::vec3 origin = -center;
        const glm::vec3 x = glm::vec3(sideLengths.x, 0.0f, 0.0f) - center;
        const glm::vec3 y = glm::vec3(0.0f, sideLengths.y, 0.0f) - center;
        const glm::vec3 z = glm::vec3(0.0f, 0.0f, sideLengths.z) - center;
        polyhedron.faces.reserve(4);
        appendFace(polyhedron, GLFace::Triangle,
            { origin, y, x }, { .9f, .4f, .3f });
        appendFace(polyhedron, GLFace::Triangle,
            { origin, x, z }, { .3f, .8f, .4f });
        appendFace(polyhedron, GLFace::Triangle,
            { origin, z, y }, { .3f, .5f, .9f });
        appendFace(polyhedron, GLFace::Triangle,
            { x, y, z }, { .9f, .8f, .3f });
    }
    return polyhedron;
}

inline Model DiagramSystem::makeSphere(float radius,unsigned slices,unsigned stacks) {
    Model model;
    if(!std::isfinite(radius)||radius<=0||slices<3||slices>512||stacks<2||stacks>256)return model;
    Mesh mesh;mesh.localVertices.push_back({0,radius,0});
    constexpr double pi=3.14159265358979323846;
    for(unsigned row=1;row<stacks;++row) {
        const double latitude=pi*row/stacks;
        for(unsigned col=0;col<slices;++col) {
            const double longitude=2*pi*col/slices;
            mesh.localVertices.push_back(radius*glm::vec3(std::sin(latitude)*std::cos(longitude),
                std::cos(latitude),std::sin(latitude)*std::sin(longitude)));
        }
    }
    const auto bottom=std::uint32_t(mesh.localVertices.size());
    mesh.localVertices.push_back({0,-radius,0});
    for(unsigned col=0;col<slices;++col) {
        const auto next=(col+1)%slices;
        mesh.triangleIndices.insert(mesh.triangleIndices.end(),{0,1+next,1+col});
        for(unsigned row=0;row+2<stacks;++row) {
            const auto a=1+row*slices+col,b=1+row*slices+next,c=a+slices,d=b+slices;
            mesh.triangleIndices.insert(mesh.triangleIndices.end(),{a,b,c,b,d,c});
        }
        const auto a=1+(stacks-2)*slices+col,b=1+(stacks-2)*slices+next;
        mesh.triangleIndices.insert(mesh.triangleIndices.end(),{a,b,bottom});
    }
    for(const auto& p:mesh.localVertices)mesh.vertexNormals.push_back(glm::normalize(p));
    model.faces.push_back(std::move(mesh));
    return validPolyhedron(model)?model:Model{};
}
inline Model DiagramSystem::makeCone(float radius,float height,unsigned slices) {
    Model model;
    if(!std::isfinite(radius)||!std::isfinite(height)||radius<=0||height<=0||slices<3||slices>512)return model;
    constexpr double pi=3.14159265358979323846;
    // Separate sides/base keep the bottom rim sharp for normals and outlines.
    Mesh sides,base;sides.localVertices.push_back({0,height*.5f,0});
    base.localVertices.push_back({0,-height*.5f,0});
    for(unsigned i=0;i<slices;++i) {
        const double a=2*pi*i/slices;
        const glm::vec3 p{radius*float(std::cos(a)),-height*.5f,radius*float(std::sin(a))};
        sides.localVertices.push_back(p);base.localVertices.push_back(p);
    }
    for(unsigned i=0;i<slices;++i) {
        const auto a=1+i,b=1+(i+1)%slices;
        sides.triangleIndices.insert(sides.triangleIndices.end(),{0,b,a});
        base.triangleIndices.insert(base.triangleIndices.end(),{0,a,b});
    }
    recalculateNormals(sides);recalculateNormals(base);
    model.faces={std::move(sides),std::move(base)};
    return validPolyhedron(model)?model:Model{};
}

inline glm::mat4 DiagramSystem::modelMatrix(const Transform3D& transform) {
    return detail::transformMatrix(transform);
}

inline glm::mat4 DiagramSystem::faceModelMatrix(const Component_Poly& component,
                                         const PlaneFigure& face) {
    return detail::faceMatrix(component, face);
}

inline bool CameraSystem::validCamera(const Camera& camera) {
    const glm::vec3 direction = camera.target - camera.position;
    const glm::vec3 right = glm::cross(direction, camera.up);
    return finiteVector(camera.position) && finiteVector(camera.target)
        && finiteVector(camera.up) && finiteVector(direction) && finiteVector(right)
        && std::isfinite(glm::dot(direction, direction))
        && std::isfinite(glm::dot(right, right))
        && glm::dot(direction, direction) > 0.000001f
        && glm::dot(right, right) > 0.000001f
        && std::isfinite(camera.fieldOfViewDegrees)
        && camera.fieldOfViewDegrees > 0.0f && camera.fieldOfViewDegrees < 180.0f
        && std::isfinite(camera.nearPlane) && std::isfinite(camera.farPlane)
        && camera.nearPlane > 0.0f && camera.farPlane > camera.nearPlane
        && std::isfinite(camera.orthographicHalfExtent)
        && camera.orthographicHalfExtent > 0.0f;
}

inline glm::mat4 CameraSystem::viewMatrix(const Camera& camera) {
    return glm::lookAt(camera.position, camera.target, camera.up);
}

inline glm::mat4 CameraSystem::projectionMatrix(const Camera& camera, float aspect) {
    if (camera.orthographic) {
        const float halfWidth = camera.orthographicHalfExtent * (aspect >= 1.0f ? aspect : 1.0f);
        const float halfHeight = camera.orthographicHalfExtent / (aspect < 1.0f ? aspect : 1.0f);
        return glm::ortho(-halfWidth, halfWidth, -halfHeight, halfHeight,
                          camera.nearPlane, camera.farPlane);
    }
    return glm::perspective(glm::radians(camera.fieldOfViewDegrees), aspect,
                            camera.nearPlane, camera.farPlane);
}


inline void Scene::rebuildFaceIds() {
    cubeFaceId_.clear(); tetraFaceId_.clear(); pyramidFaceId_.clear();
    for (EntityId id : polyId_) {
        const auto& model = components_[id].polyhedron;
        auto* ids = model.shape == GLShape::Cube ? &cubeFaceId_
            : model.shape == GLShape::Tetra ? &tetraFaceId_
            : model.shape == GLShape::Pyramid ? &pyramidFaceId_ : nullptr;
        if(ids) for(std::size_t f=0; f<model.faces.size(); ++f) ids->push_back({id,f});
    }
}
inline std::optional<EntityId> Scene::addModel(const Model& model, const Transform3D& transform,
                                             PivotMode pivot, const glm::vec3& customPivot) {
    if(!DiagramSystem::validPolyhedron(model) || !detail::finiteVector(customPivot)) return (Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::addModel", "Rejected condition: !DiagramSystem::validPolyhedron(model) || !detail::finiteVector(customPivot)", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships."), std::nullopt);
    auto initial=transform;
    if(pivot==PivotMode::BottomCenter) initial.localBasis=detail::bottomCenter(model);
    else if(pivot==PivotMode::Custom) initial.localBasis=customPivot;
    auto id=push_entity(model,initial);
    if(id) components_[*id].pivotMode=pivot;
    return id;
}
inline bool Scene::setModel(EntityId id, const Model& model) {
    if(id>=components_.size() || !DiagramSystem::validPolyhedron(model)) return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::setModel", "Rejected condition: id>=components_.size() || !DiagramSystem::validPolyhedron(model)", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id);
    const auto box=detail::bounds(model);
    if(!detail::finiteVector(box.second-box.first)) return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::setModel", "Rejected condition: !detail::finiteVector(box.second-box.first)", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id);
    auto replacement=components_[id];
    replacement.polyhedron=model;replacement.sideLengths=box.second-box.first;replacement.transformOnly=false;
    if(replacement.pivotMode==PivotMode::BottomCenter)
        replacement.transform.localBasis=detail::bottomCenter(model);
    components_[id]=std::move(replacement);
    rebuildFaceIds();geometryRevision_=nextGeometryRevision();return true;
}
inline const Mesh* Scene::getMesh(EntityId id, std::size_t meshIndex) const {
    const auto* c=getComponent(id);
    return c && meshIndex<c->polyhedron.faces.size()?&c->polyhedron.faces[meshIndex]:nullptr;
}
inline bool Scene::setMesh(EntityId id, std::size_t meshIndex, const Mesh& mesh) {
    if(!getMesh(id,meshIndex)) return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::setMesh", "Rejected condition: !getMesh(id,meshIndex)", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id);
    Model replacement=components_[id].polyhedron;
    replacement.faces[meshIndex]=mesh;
    return setModel(id,replacement);
}
inline bool Scene::setMeshVertices(EntityId id,std::size_t meshIndex,
                                   const std::vector<Vertex>& vertices,
                                   const std::vector<std::uint32_t>& triangles) {
    const auto* original=getMesh(id,meshIndex);if(!original)return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::setMeshVertices", "Rejected condition: !original", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id);
    auto replacement=*original;
    const auto geometry=Mesh::fromVertices(vertices,triangles);
    replacement.localVertices=geometry.localVertices;replacement.vertexNormals=geometry.vertexNormals;
    replacement.texcoords=geometry.texcoords;replacement.vertexColors=geometry.vertexColors;
    replacement.triangleIndices=triangles;replacement.shape=GLFace::Mesh;
    replacement.outlineIndices.clear();
    if(replacement.outline==OutlineMode::Explicit) replacement.outline=OutlineMode::None;
    return setMesh(id,meshIndex,replacement);
}
inline bool Scene::setVertexPosition(EntityId id,std::size_t meshIndex,std::size_t vertexIndex,
                                     const glm::vec3& position) {
    const auto* mesh=getMesh(id,meshIndex);
    if(!mesh||vertexIndex>=mesh->localVertices.size()||!detail::finiteVector(position))return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::setVertexPosition", "Rejected condition: !mesh||vertexIndex>=mesh->localVertices.size()||!detail::finiteVector(position)", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id);
    auto replacement=*mesh;replacement.localVertices[vertexIndex]=position;
    recalculateNormals(replacement);
    return setMesh(id,meshIndex,replacement);
}
inline bool Scene::setTriangleIndices(EntityId id,std::size_t meshIndex,
                                      const std::vector<std::uint32_t>& indices) {
    const auto* mesh=getMesh(id,meshIndex);if(!mesh)return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::setTriangleIndices", "Rejected condition: !mesh", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id);
    auto replacement=*mesh;replacement.shape=GLFace::Mesh;replacement.triangleIndices=indices;
    if(!detail::validMesh(replacement))return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::setTriangleIndices", "Rejected condition: !detail::validMesh(replacement)", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id);
    recalculateNormals(replacement);return setMesh(id,meshIndex,replacement);
}
inline bool Scene::generateTexcoords(EntityId id, std::size_t meshIndex, const UVOptions& options) {
    const auto* mesh=getMesh(id,meshIndex); if(!mesh)return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::generateTexcoords", "Rejected condition: !mesh", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id);
    auto replacement=*mesh;
    return graphics::generateTexcoords(replacement,options) && setMesh(id,meshIndex,replacement);
}
inline bool Scene::transformTexcoords(EntityId id, std::size_t meshIndex, const glm::vec2& scale,
                                     const glm::vec2& offset, float rotationDegrees) {
    const auto* mesh=getMesh(id,meshIndex); if(!mesh)return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::transformTexcoords", "Rejected condition: !mesh", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id);
    auto replacement=*mesh;
    return graphics::transformTexcoords(replacement,scale,offset,rotationDegrees) && setMesh(id,meshIndex,replacement);
}
inline bool Scene::setTexcoords(EntityId id,std::size_t meshIndex,const std::vector<glm::vec2>& uvs) {
    const auto* mesh=getMesh(id,meshIndex);if(!mesh)return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::setTexcoords", "Rejected condition: !mesh", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id);
    auto replacement=*mesh;replacement.texcoords=uvs;return setMesh(id,meshIndex,replacement);
}
inline bool Scene::setVertexNormals(EntityId id,std::size_t meshIndex,const std::vector<glm::vec3>& normals) {
    const auto* mesh=getMesh(id,meshIndex);if(!mesh)return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::setVertexNormals", "Rejected condition: !mesh", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id);
    auto replacement=*mesh;replacement.vertexNormals=normals;return setMesh(id,meshIndex,replacement);
}
inline bool Scene::setMaterial(EntityId id,std::size_t meshIndex,const Material& material) {
    const auto* mesh=getMesh(id,meshIndex);if(!mesh)return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::setMaterial", "Rejected condition: !mesh", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id);
    auto replacement=*mesh;replacement.material=material;return setMesh(id,meshIndex,replacement);
}
inline bool Scene::setOutline(EntityId id,std::size_t meshIndex,OutlineMode mode,
                              const std::vector<std::uint32_t>& explicitEdges) {
    const auto* mesh=getMesh(id,meshIndex);if(!mesh)return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::setOutline", "Rejected condition: !mesh", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id);
    auto replacement=*mesh;replacement.outline=mode;replacement.outlineIndices=explicitEdges;
    return setMesh(id,meshIndex,replacement);
}
inline bool Scene::setLocalBasis(EntityId id,PivotMode mode,const glm::vec3& customPivot) {
    if(id>=components_.size()||!detail::finiteVector(customPivot))return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::setLocalBasis", "Rejected condition: id>=components_.size()||!detail::finiteVector(customPivot)", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id);
    auto& c=components_[id];
    if(c.transformOnly && mode==PivotMode::BottomCenter)return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::setLocalBasis", "Rejected condition: c.transformOnly && mode==PivotMode::BottomCenter", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id);
    if(mode==PivotMode::BottomCenter)c.transform.localBasis=detail::bottomCenter(c.polyhedron);
    else if(mode==PivotMode::Custom)c.transform.localBasis=customPivot;
    c.pivotMode=mode;return true;
}
inline bool Scene::setVisible(EntityId id,bool visible) {
    if(id>=components_.size())return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::setVisible", "Rejected condition: id>=components_.size()", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id);
    components_[id].visible=visible;return true;
}
inline bool Scene::splitFace(EntityId id,std::size_t faceIndex) {
    if(!getMesh(id,faceIndex))return Diagnostics::failAt(__FILE__,__LINE__,PipelineStage::Scene, "Scene::splitFace", "Rejected condition: !getMesh(id,faceIndex)", "Check finite values, valid IDs, dimensions, indices and parent/layer relationships.", id);
    // The same face remains under its parent; vertices and indices are already face-local storage.
    auto& face=components_[id].polyhedron.faces[faceIndex];
    if(!face.independence) {face.transform=Transform3D{};face.independence=true;}
    return true;
}
inline bool Scene::setFaceSRT(EntityId id,std::size_t faceIndex,const Transform3D& transform) {
    return setFaceTransform(id,faceIndex,transform,true);
}
} // namespace graphics

함수·반환값·실패 조건과 함께 읽기

면 힌지와 추가 로컬 행렬

몸체의 자식 관절을 만들고 문을 열거나 카메라를 돌린다. 이 함수들은 응용의 키 입력이나 누적 진행률을 Scene·Camera 상태로 바꾸는 작은 재사용 도구다.

주요 상태: anchor는 부모 공간의 관절 위치다. progress는 0~1 개방률이며 dt가 아니다. cameraFacingWall의 결과는 선택한 옆벽 번호다. selected가 비면 벽을 임의로 열지 않는다.

실제 응용 코드 묶음

새로 작성한 사용 사례. 같은 API를 쓰는 기존 실행 소스도 아래에 전체 첨부한다.

bool editParts(graphics::Scene& scene,graphics::EntityId body,graphics::EntityId room,graphics::Camera& camera) {
    auto joint=graphics::PatternSystem::addJoint(scene,body,{0,.5f,0});
    if(!joint||!graphics::PatternSystem::rotateJoint(scene,*joint,{1,0,0},30)) return false;
    auto polygon=graphics::PatternSystem::addPolygon(scene,{{0,0,0},{1,0,0},{0,1,0}},{1,1,0},body);
    if(!polygon||!graphics::PatternSystem::addNumber(scene,3,{0,1,0},.2f,{1,1,1},body)) return false;
    if(!graphics::PatternSystem::foldFace(scene,body,0,{{0,.5f,0},{1,0,0}},45)) return false;
    if(!graphics::PatternSystem::openFace(scene,body,0,.5f,{0,1,0},{0,.5f,0},true)) return false;
    auto selected=graphics::PatternSystem::cameraFacingWall(scene,room,camera);
    if(selected&&!graphics::PatternSystem::openRoomWall(scene,room,*selected,.5f)) return false;
    auto other=scene.addCuboid({1,1,1},{2,0,0}); if(!other) return false;
    if(!graphics::PatternSystem::swapModels(scene,body,*other)) return false;
    if(!graphics::PatternSystem::moveCamera(camera,{.2f,0,0})
       ||!graphics::PatternSystem::yawCamera(camera,5)
       ||!graphics::PatternSystem::orbitCamera(camera,{{0,0,0},{0,1,0}},5)
       ||!graphics::PatternSystem::zoomCamera(camera,.9f)) return false;
    auto views=graphics::PatternSystem::threeViews(camera,1100,800); return !views.empty();
}

이 단편의 각 조작은 별도의 입력 명령 사례다. 실제 앱에서는 같은 면을 foldFace와 openFace가 동시에 덮어쓰지 않게 모드를 정한다. 벽 표시 개방은 충돌 경계 제거와 별개다.

실제 저장소 소스 · openGL_3dAssign_22/Console.cpp
#include "Console.h"
#include <algorithm>
#include <cmath>
#include <iostream>
#include <stdexcept>

namespace {
graphics::EntityId requireEntity(std::optional<graphics::EntityId> id,const char* reason) {
    if(!id)throw std::runtime_error(reason);
    return *id;
}
}
graphics::EntityId Console::box(glm::vec3 size,glm::vec3 position,graphics::EntityId parent) {
    const auto id=requireEntity(graphics::PatternSystem::addBox(
        app_.getScene(),size,position,{1,1,1},parent),"탱크 상자 생성 실패: 크기·위치·부모 ID를 확인하세요.");
    colored_.push_back(id);
    return id;
}
bool Console::initScene() {
    reset();
    std::cout << "실습 22: 같은 장면을 원근 / XZ 평면도 / XY 정면도로 표시\n"
              << "방향키 이동; d 하부 높이; t 중앙 자전; l 상부 교환\n"
              << "g 포신 반대 회전; p 깃대 반대 기울기; x/X,z/Z 카메라 이동\n"
              << "y/Y 카메라 시선 회전; r/R 공전(재입력 정지); O/o 색 변경; c 초기화; q 종료\n";
    return true;
}
void Console::reset() {
    auto& scene=app_.getScene();
    scene.clear();
    colored_.clear();
    pressed_.fill(false);
    position_=glm::vec3(0);
    lowerTime_=centralAngle_=gunAngle_=poleTime_=swapProgress_=0;
    swapDirection_=1;
    cameraX_=cameraZ_=cameraYaw_=cameraOrbit_=0;
    lowerRunning_=centralRunning_=gunRunning_=poleRunning_=swapRunning_=swapStarted_=false;
    colorVersion_=0;
    root_=requireEntity(scene.addGroup(),"탱크 루트 생성 실패.");
    central_=requireEntity(graphics::PatternSystem::addJoint(scene,root_,{0,.6f,0}),
        "중앙 관절 생성 실패.");
    const auto ground=requireEntity(scene.addGroup(),"바닥 그룹 생성 실패.");
    box({12,.1f,12},{0,-.05f,0},ground);
    for(std::size_t i=0;i<2;++i)lower_[i]=box({.65f,.6f,1.8f},{i==0?-.8f:.8f,.3f,0},root_);
    box({2.3f,.25f,1.4f},{0,.125f,0},central_);
    for(std::size_t i=0;i<2;++i) {
        const float sign=i==0?-1.0f:1.0f;
        upper_[i]=requireEntity(graphics::PatternSystem::addJoint(scene,central_,{sign*.65f,.25f,0}),
            "상부 관절 생성 실패.");
        guns_[i]=requireEntity(graphics::PatternSystem::addJoint(scene,upper_[i],{0,.3f,0}),
            "포신 중심 피벗 생성 실패.");
        poles_[i]=requireEntity(graphics::PatternSystem::addJoint(scene,upper_[i],{0,.6f,0}),
            "깃대 밑점 피벗 생성 실패.");
        box({.65f,.6f,.8f},{0,.3f,0},upper_[i]);

        box({.14f,.14f,1.1f},{0,-.05f,.95f},guns_[i]);
        box({.08f,.85f,.08f},{0,.425f,0},poles_[i]);
    }
    auto& camera=app_.getCamera();
    camera=graphics::Camera{};
    camera.position={-5,4.5f,7};
    camera.target={0,.7f,0};
    camera.orthographic=false;
    camera.orthographicHalfExtent=6.5f;
    app_.getRenderOptions()=graphics::RenderOptions{};
    app_.getRenderOptions().lighting=true;
    recolor();
    apply();
}
void Console::recolor() {
    const std::array<graphics::GlColor,10> palette{
        graphics::GlColor{.3f,.35f,.4f},{.25f,.6f,.3f},{.55f,.35f,.2f},{.2f,.55f,.75f},
        {.7f,.75f,.2f},{.9f,.4f,.15f},{1,.8f,.2f},{.4f,.7f,.8f},{.7f,.25f,.6f},{.6f,.9f,.4f}};
    for(std::size_t i=0;i<colored_.size();++i)
        if(!app_.getScene().setTint(colored_[i],palette[(i+colorVersion_)%palette.size()]))
            throw std::runtime_error("탱크 부품 색상 적용 실패.");
}
void Console::apply() {
    auto& scene=app_.getScene();
    if(!scene.setPosition(root_,position_))throw std::runtime_error("탱크 위치 적용 실패.");
    const float factor=1+.25f*std::sin(lowerTime_),height=.6f*factor;
    if(!scene.setPosition(central_,{0,height,0})
        ||!graphics::PatternSystem::rotateJoint(scene,central_,{0,1,0},centralAngle_))
        throw std::runtime_error("중앙 관절 변환 적용 실패.");
    for(std::size_t i=0;i<2;++i) {
        const float sign=i==0?-1.0f:1.0f;
        graphics::Transform3D low;
        low.basis={sign*.8f,height*.5f,0};
        low.scale={1,factor,1};
        const float a=3.14159265f*swapProgress_;
        const glm::vec3 upperPosition{sign*.65f*std::cos(a),.25f,sign*.65f*std::sin(a)};
        if(!scene.setTransform(lower_[i],low)||!scene.setPosition(upper_[i],upperPosition)
            ||!graphics::PatternSystem::rotateJoint(scene,guns_[i],{0,1,0},sign*gunAngle_)
            ||!graphics::PatternSystem::rotateJoint(scene,poles_[i],{1,0,0},sign*60*std::sin(poleTime_)))
            throw std::runtime_error("탱크 부품 변환 적용 실패.");
    }
}
void Console::onKey(int key,int action,int mods) {
    const bool upper=((mods&GLFW_MOD_SHIFT)!=0)!=((mods&GLFW_MOD_CAPS_LOCK)!=0);
    if(key==GLFW_KEY_X||key==GLFW_KEY_Z) {
        if(action==GLFW_PRESS||action==GLFW_RELEASE) {
            float& direction=key==GLFW_KEY_X?cameraX_:cameraZ_;
            direction=action==GLFW_RELEASE?0:(upper?-1.0f:1.0f);
        }
        return;
    }
    if(key>=0&&key<=GLFW_KEY_LAST) {
        if(action==GLFW_PRESS)pressed_[key]=true;
        else if(action==GLFW_RELEASE)pressed_[key]=false;
    }
    if(action!=GLFW_PRESS)return;
    const float direction=upper?1.0f:-1.0f;
    switch(key) {
    case GLFW_KEY_D:
        lowerRunning_=!lowerRunning_;
        break;
    case GLFW_KEY_T:
        centralRunning_=!centralRunning_;
        break;
    case GLFW_KEY_G:
        gunRunning_=!gunRunning_;
        break;
    case GLFW_KEY_P:
        poleRunning_=!poleRunning_;
        break;
    case GLFW_KEY_L:
        if(!swapStarted_){
            swapStarted_=true;
            swapRunning_=true;
        }
        else if(!swapRunning_&&(swapProgress_==0||swapProgress_==1)){
            swapDirection_=-swapDirection_;
            swapRunning_=true;
        }
        else swapRunning_=!swapRunning_;
        break;
    case GLFW_KEY_Y:
        cameraYaw_=cameraYaw_==direction?0:direction;
        break;
    case GLFW_KEY_R:
        cameraOrbit_=cameraOrbit_==direction?0:direction;
        break;
    case GLFW_KEY_O:
        colorVersion_=(colorVersion_+1)%10;
        recolor();
        break;
    case GLFW_KEY_C:
        reset();
        break;
    case GLFW_KEY_Q:
    case GLFW_KEY_ESCAPE:
        app_.requestClose();
        break;
    default:
        break;
    }
}
void Console::onUpdate(float dt) {
    glm::vec3 movement{float(pressed_[GLFW_KEY_RIGHT])-float(pressed_[GLFW_KEY_LEFT]),0,
        float(pressed_[GLFW_KEY_DOWN])-float(pressed_[GLFW_KEY_UP])};
    if(glm::length(movement)>0)position_+=glm::normalize(movement)*dt*1.3f;

    position_.x=std::clamp(position_.x,-3.5f,3.5f);
    position_.z=std::clamp(position_.z,-3.5f,3.5f);
    if(lowerRunning_)lowerTime_=std::fmod(lowerTime_+dt,6.283185307f);
    if(centralRunning_)centralAngle_=std::fmod(centralAngle_+45*dt,360.0f);
    if(gunRunning_)gunAngle_= std::fmod(gunAngle_+60*dt,-360.0f);
    if(poleRunning_)poleTime_=std::fmod(poleTime_+dt,6.283185307f);
    if(swapRunning_) {
        swapProgress_=std::clamp(swapProgress_+swapDirection_*dt*.5f,0.0f,1.0f);
        if(swapProgress_==0||swapProgress_==1)swapRunning_=false;
    }
    auto camera=app_.getCamera();
    const glm::vec3 delta{cameraX_*2*dt,0,cameraZ_*2*dt};
    if(!graphics::PatternSystem::moveCamera(camera,delta)
        ||(cameraYaw_!=0&&!graphics::PatternSystem::yawCamera(camera,cameraYaw_*35*dt))
        ||(cameraOrbit_!=0&&!graphics::PatternSystem::orbitCamera(camera,{{0,0,0},{0,1,0}},cameraOrbit_*25*dt)))
        throw std::runtime_error("카메라 갱신 실패: 위치·시선·회전축을 확인하세요.");
    app_.getCamera()=camera;
    apply();
}
void Console::onFocus(bool focused) {
    if(!focused){
        pressed_.fill(false);
        cameraX_=cameraZ_=0;
    }
}

bool Console::onRender() {
    const auto size=app_.getFramebufferSize();
    if(size[0]<3||size[1]<2)return true;
    const auto passes=graphics::PatternSystem::threeViews(
        app_.getCamera(),size[0],size[1],app_.getRenderOptions());
    if(passes.size()!=3)throw std::runtime_error("세 카메라 뷰 생성 실패: 프레임버퍼 크기와 카메라를 확인하세요.");
    return app_.renderViews(passes);
}
실제 저장소 소스 · openGL_3dAssign_23/Console.cpp
#include "Console.h"
#include <algorithm>
#include <stdexcept>
#include <iostream>
namespace {
void require(bool ok,const char* message) {if(!ok) throw std::runtime_error(message);}
}


bool Console::initScene() {
    auto& scene=app_.getScene();scene.clear();obstacles_.clear();
    body_=graphics::CharacterBody{};body_.feet={-3,0,-3};body_.speed=1;
    body_.height=1.8f;body_.halfWidth={.5f,.5f};
    doors_.fill(0);open_.fill(false);activeWall_.reset();walking_=true;
    const auto stage=graphics::PatternSystem::addRoom(scene,bounds_,false);
    if(!stage) return false;stage_=*stage;
    for(std::size_t face=0;face<4;++face) {
        if(!graphics::PatternSystem::openRoomWall(scene,stage_,face,0)) return false;
    }
    graphics::GridLayout grid;grid.origin={-.5f,0,-.3f};grid.extent={1,1.8f,.6f};
